ses, işitilmez olduğu için değil, akustik gücü anlamlı hitabı örttüğü için iletişim bakımından tanınmaz hale gelir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000356/B004","root_000890/B001","root_001550/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_369b42cb8e877f6e6000","text":"2. **The most unrecognized voice defeats communication by excess.** **ن ك ر B001/B003** supplies non-recognition and the severe unfamiliar thing; **ص و ت B001** is voice; **ح م ر B004** identifies the donkey. The superlative does not deny audibility; it marks a sound whose force makes it least assimilable as meaningful social address. I infer that unmodulated output crosses from communication into aversive signal. The animal comparison diagnoses failed recognition, while remaining anchored to the stated sound.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_3963eab2a3f166d1757a","text":"- 31:22's firm grip corrected any reading of moderation as weak commitment: pace and volume may be measured while attachment remains strongest. 31:24's contrast between little enjoyment and thick punishment also made the ayah's concern with proportion and scale newly visible.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000890/B001","root_001093/B001","root_001230/B001","root_001427/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_8820ccb037bb14056602","text":"1. **Aim produces moderation in both motion and sound.** **ق ص د B001–B003** is aiming, straight gait, and a middle between extremes; **م ش ي B001** walking; **غ ض ض B001** restraining by lowering; **ص و ت B001** audible voicing. Gait receives positive aiming while voice receives measured reduction. I infer a shared calibration mechanism: bodily output is adjusted to purpose rather than maximized. The ayah reads moderation as directed precision, not bland middleness.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000356/B004","root_000890/B001","root_001550/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_acb3730f278f83a6e85b","text":"2. **An unrecognizable voice as failed communication.** Lexical evidence: `ن ك ر B003/B004` is the severe, unfamiliar thing and alteration into a disliked state; `ح م ر B004` is the donkey; `ص و ت B001` includes voice, noise, and clamor. Structural cue: the sound root occurs three times, moving from the addressee's voice through all voices to the concrete comparison. Assigned role: repetition isolates acoustic form, and the donkey comparison supplies the terminal image of uncontrolled voicing. Abductive move (inferred): excessive sound can deform speech until its communicative content is eclipsed by its acoustic force. Reading change: `أَنكَرَ ٱلْأَصْوَٰتِ` diagnoses a loss of communicative recognizability, not only an unpleasant timbre.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001230/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_b442f7b6ac0209e58a25","text":"Ayah 31:32’s *muqtaṣid* unexpectedly transferred **ق ص د B002–B004** from bodily calibration to post-crisis fidelity. Moderation therefore became a general technology of sustainable direction, not only etiquette of gait and volume.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001230/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_d29cb5db0214fcf610f1","text":"1. **One calibration governing movement and sound.** Lexical evidence: `ق ص د B002/B003` is straightness of gait and moderation between extremes, `م ش ي B001` is movement, `غ ض ض B001` is restraining by lessening, and `ص و ت B001` is audible voice. Structural cue: two parallel imperatives regulate pace and volume, following 31:18's excessive gait and 31:17's command to public speech. Assigned role: purpose and moderation set bodily pace; measured lowering sets vocal reach. Abductive move (inferred): public ethical action remains sound only when both bodily and acoustic presence are proportioned to their task. Reading change: `ٱقْصِدْ` supplies a cross-channel discipline of presence, not merely a preferred walking speed.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
